{"forms":[{"form":"the Petersen-Morley theorem","tags":["canonical"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["A theorem stating that, if a, b, c are three general skew lines in space, if a′, b′, c′ are the lines of shortest distance respectively for the pairs (b,c), (c,a) and (a,b), and if p, q and r are the lines of shortest distance respectively for the pairs (a,a′), (b,b′) and (c,c′), then there is a single line meeting at right angles all of p, q, and r."],"topics":["geometry","mathematics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":9163506,"upstream_offset":22347513538,"upstream_record_sha256":"d766ae4d9fd6ee9a79a5ea039c5eb01af3ffc6f96efc5e6a2f1d915a37546b27","word":"Petersen-Morley theorem"}

to rid of impurities; to cleanse; to clear of (something unwanted)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To remove by cleansing; to wash away."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To free from sin, guilt, or burden."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To evacuate (the bowels or the stomach); to defecate or vomit."],"tags":["intransitive","transitive"],"topics":["medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["To cause someone to purge; to operate (on somebody) using a cathartic or emetic, or in a similar manner."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["medicine","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Deng Xiaoping was purged twice during the Cultural Revolution, but managed to return to power after Mao's death.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To forcibly remove, for example, from political activity."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Cromwell had Colonel Pride purge Parliament of royalists who opposed Charles I's execution.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To forcibly remove people by an organization."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To clear of a charge, suspicion, or imputation."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["law"]},{"glosses":["To clarify;
